| No native session id, token or unredacted output in commits, evidence, API responses or indexes | Passed for the checks listed | API responses and indexes: the project fixture's leak check after every Projects integration case (responses, views and hub indexes), the slim thread index (never a working directory, native session or transcript) and the bounded redaction of decisions 563 and 577. Evidence: no text file under docs/acceptance holds a bare UUID-shaped value (the only UUIDs there sit inside Jevellan `work_` ids), a token shape, an `Authorization` value or a real native session path. Commits: the secret scan covers every commit in history and the pre-push hook each pushed one; the evidence scan above covers the files this phase adds. Only the loopback attach answer carries a native session id, by design (decision 534). |
| REPORT.md lists what is live, simulated and not run | Listed | The list below, the Projects phase progress table, the phase bullets under Current state and the PJ documents. |
| REPORT.md lists every decision taken where the brief was silent | Listed | Decisions 236 to 591, each with the build design's id; the deliberate deviations are decisions 238, 244, 250, 255, 299, 448, 488, 564 and 579. |
| REPORT.md records the deployment state | Recorded | The deployment line below. |
| The owner's acceptance is not claimed | Not claimed | Gonçalo's acceptance of the Projects build is neither requested nor claimed. |

What the Projects evidence is:

- **Live:** Git with local bare origins (worktrees, branches, rebases, conflicts and pushes); the daemons with their HTTP and SSE routes, the bridge and the loopback control routes; the hub's SQLite store and relay; checkout ownership and publication leases; process groups and signals, and the attach command as a real process, also in a real pseudo-terminal; Chromium in four layouts. The installed `claude` and `codex` ran only to print their help (decision 540).
- **Simulated:** coordinator and thread turns (FakeRuntime scripts that call the real bridge), the provider CLIs for the turn adapters and for attach (fixture executables), Jev's placement answers (a scripted transport and the fixture server's fake Jev), GitHub (a local fake server behind a GitHub-shaped remote) and the second device (a member daemon on the same machine).
- **Not run or blocked:** PJ-live (blocked by ENV-JEV, ENV-CODEX and ENV-GITHUB, ENV-CLAUDE optional; only its blocked receipt exists); the automated vision checks on this machine (ENV-CLAUDE); the live Jev tests (4 skipped, ENV-JEV); live Claude and Codex turns and terminal resumes, including the Claude retention setting of decision 564 against a live CLI; a real GitHub pull request, its checks and its merge; a second physical device (DEVICE); a real hub outage.
- **Projects vision checks: pending.** The automated vision checks for the Projects journeys run on dev-madrid, which holds the dedicated test credential (`JEVELLAN_TEST_CLAUDE_TOKEN`), after the final commit. Until their result is recorded on this line, every Projects vision record says not run.
- **Projects deployment: pending.** The deployment to the existing Mac mini installation with the established update flow, and the check that accounts, projects, conversation ledgers and settings are preserved, are recorded on this line when done.

## Acceptance journeys

[J6](J6.md) passed the live invalid-key recovery journey. [J1](J1.md) and [J2](J2.md) preserve their live manual results; their full automatic forms and [J3](J3.md), [J4](J4.md), [J5](J5.md), [J7](J7.md) and [J13](J13.md) still require actual successful Jev classification. Those reports map the related component/integration/browser evidence and remaining live work. [J8](J8.md) and the complete cross-device [J11](J11.md) now pass the permitted local simulated-device journeys with manual choices; installation and later phases remain unfinished. No simulated journey is claimed as live and no unimplemented feature is classified as an environmental blocker. Projects [PJ1](PJ1.md) and PJ1b to PJ1f pass as simulated integration and adapter tests, [PJ2](PJ2.md) and PJ2b to PJ2e as simulated integration tests, [PJ3](PJ3.md) as simulated browser journeys in four layouts with the vision checks not run, [PJ4](PJ4.md) and PJ4b as simulated integration tests with a scripted Jev and a simulated browser journey in four layouts with the vision checks not run, and [PJ5](PJ5.md) as a simulated integration test with a member daemon on this machine and two simulated browser journeys in four layouts with the vision checks not run, and [PJ6](PJ6.md) as a simulated integration test with a member daemon on this machine and one bare origin, its interface changes covered by the PJ3 and PJ4b browser journeys with the vision checks not run, and [PJ7](PJ7.md) as a simulated integration test that runs the real command with simulated provider CLIs on PATH and a simulated browser journey in four layouts with the vision checks not run; all of them passed again in the final phase 8 runs. The live Projects journey [PJ-live](PJ-live.md) is blocked (ENV-JEV, ENV-CODEX, ENV-GITHUB) and has not run live.

## Next work

The original BRIEF.md build paused on 2026-09-25 with a usable pilot (see [PILOT.md](PILOT.md) for the running app, setup and exact evidence limits) and resumed on 2026-09-26 on dev-madrid, which completed the local checks of phases 6 and 7 with the blockers in the Phase progress table; the original section-20 definition of done has not been met (see its audit above). The Projects build (BRIEF-projects.md) completed phases 1 to 8 with simulated evidence, with the blockers in the [Projects definition of done audit](#projects-definition-of-done-audit). Still to do there: the automated vision checks for the Projects journeys on dev-madrid and the deployment to the Mac mini, each on its own line in that audit. Checks that need credentials or hardware stay blocked, never passed, until they arrive: PJ-live (the dedicated Jev key, a Codex API key of a test project and a disposable GitHub repository with its token), the live Jev tests, live Claude and Codex turns and terminal resumes, and a second physical device. The GitHub token help line in Settings now names Contents write and Commit statuses read as well, because GitHub lists merging a pull request under Contents write and the combined commit status under Commit statuses read; the specification's verbatim line would have produced a refused merge and pending checks (decision 579, a deliberate deviation; unverified live, and PJ-live asks its test token for both permissions, decision 551). Known Projects gaps, recorded and not fixed: TH-6, C-5 and C-6 (decision 575) and N-3 (decision 565) from the reviews; a restart onto main relayed from a coordinator on another device is refused on the old thread's device (decision 529); an agent that leaves the checkout off main parks its thread without telling the coordinator (decision 561); a stop settlement retried without a record of what its refusal saw (after a failed release, an unconfirmed process or a hub outage) can still commit owner edits made on main into the discard ref, where they are kept (decision 576); an agent report that redaction would lengthen past its maximum is refused to the agent (decision 563); conversations do not respect an attached thread's account hold, and a turn already running when the owner attaches keeps running (decision 554); member reads sent as POST still pass the lifecycle gate (decision 471); and a thread page whose device goes offline while it is open keeps its actions enabled, each refused with the device sentence, beside the last state it read (decision 508). Later improvements are the planned items in `roadmap.json`, including every non-goal of the Projects specification.
